Model before rewriting:

find a: int(5..8)
find b: int(5..8)
find c: int(9..17)
find d: int(-2..1)
find e: int(7..9)

such that

sum([a,b;int(1..)]) = c,
sum([sum([c,d;int(1..)]),e;int(1..)]) > 16
